Meetings

Devices

##  The basics 
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/calls_icon.png)
[Make calls](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/9kx9h9hw6bvfnhvggqwbx95/How_to_make_a_call_Quick_Start_Guide)
Look for the audio or video call icons throughout the Webex App.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/answer_calls_icon.png)
[Answer calls](https://help.webex.com/xga73p/)
Accept or decline a call with a tap of a button on any device.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/transfer_calls_icon.png)
[Transfer calls](https://help.webex.com/5er4f3/)
Easily send an active call to someone else.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/call-forward_icon.png)
[Forward calls](https://help.webex.com/n6ycytf/)
Forward calls to another number to stay connected when you’re away.
* * *
##  Seamless communication 
Choose your favorites
It’s easy to connect with the people you talk to most. Add your boss, teammates, or customers to your contacts list and call with a click.
[ Add contacts ](https://help.webex.com/z3397j/)
Never miss a call
That’s what voicemail is for! When you have a new voicemail, you’ll see a red icon letting you know how many messages are waiting.
[ Manage voicemail ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/nzt858bj73j6g7rmhg6qt/Webex_App_voicemail_QSG)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Calling_UI.png)
### Ready for more?
Use our advanced calling features and make your workday more seamless.
[See all advanced features](https://help.webex.com/nfylvfcb/)  
[Hold and park calls](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/xpts6jw79wrbc5jgwcq7f/Call_hold_and_call_park)  
[Manage multiple lines](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/b9j9wgg2g79ktrpt6gchvp4/Calling_with_multiple_lines_QSG)  
[Record a call](https://help.webex.com/nuu13a1 "Record a call")  
[Review AI-generated summaries](https://help.webex.com/9leme "Review AI-generated summaries")
##  Adoption guides 
Whatever stage you’re at, we’ve got use cases, interactive templates, and proven strategies to help drive adoption.
[![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Plan_2x.png)](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/29mb2kn4rscwsqwrrj34p56/Calling_Adoption_Plan_Guidebook)
[Plan](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/29mb2kn4rscwsqwrrj34p56/Calling_Adoption_Plan_Guidebook)
Define your business outcomes and set up Webex Calling services
* * *
[![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Launch_2x.png)](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/7c6nvj2psrxqk74556p864n5/Calling_Adoption_Launch_Guidebook)
[Launch](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/7c6nvj2psrxqk74556p864n5/Calling_Adoption_Launch_Guidebook)
Onboard and train users, create champions, and track your success
* * *
[![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Grow_2x.png)](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/wfqrfngg4q9z9bx4c85v3j98/Calling_Adoption_Grow_Guidebook)
[Grow](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/wfqrfngg4q9z9bx4c85v3j98/Calling_Adoption_Grow_Guidebook)
Maximize your investment with advanced features and integrations
* * *
##  Admin launch kits 
Once you’ve got the basics, use these handy kits to roll out features that level up your calling experience.
[ Calling launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/chwgkwb4p84c5wkwpwrrpmw)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/launch_kit_black.png)
[Auto attendant](https://resources.webex.com/s/mxhwp5jh3snmtjq86qbjg77)
Enable auto attendant to manage calls when receptionists are unavailable.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/launch_kit_black.png)
[Live answer](https://resources.webex.com/s/n8jc9gq3z2x4tjwnp3wtzn5)
Use live answer to automate your customer service and improve customer experience.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/launch_kit_black.png)
[Call queue](https://resources.webex.com/s/nwvwx83k8mjr64prrp5z7ntb)
Set up call queue for a seamless call waiting step that suits your organization and call volume.
* * *

Get started

[ Learn more ](https://adopt.webex.com/use-the-webex-suite/do-more/best-practices "Best practices")

##  Start with the basics 
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Scedule_Meeting_icon.png)
[Schedule](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/k7m4s8qt5q8w8f4mf6vtmxf8/Webex_Scheduler_-_Quick_Start_Guide)
Set up a future meeting or series from the Meetings tab  
or Outlook.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Start_Meeting_icon.png)
[Start](https://help.webex.com/ngft4sdb/)
Meet on the fly in your Personal Room. Just share a link!
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Join_Meeting_icon.png)
[Join](https://help.webex.com/k7cstu/)
There are lots of ways to join a meeting. Which is best for you?
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Share_screen_icon.png)
[Share](https://help.webex.com/yl90d9/)
Get everyone on the same page by sharing a file or your entire screen.
* * *
## Meet smarter with  
AI Assistant
[ See quick start guide ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/jkg82sjk2hx6m353369mhp7j/AI_Assistant_Meeting_QSG)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/101124_Meetings_AI_1.png)
[During the meeting](https://help.webex.com/article/nyzkuik/Webex-App-|-Cisco-AI-Assistant-in-meetings)
Late or stepped away? Get a live recap of what you missed—action items, mentions of your name, and more.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/101124_Meetings_AI_2.png)
[After the meeting](https://help.webex.com/article/nslyqzx/Webex-App-|-AI-generated-meeting-summaries-in-recordings)
AI-generated meeting summaries include chapters, notes, and action items—so nobody misses a thing.
##  Be seen and heard 
Smart audio options
Adjust your audio options to optimize for your voice or multiple voices, using headphones, playing music, and more.
[ Manage audio ](https://help.webex.com/ela6i8/)
Virtual backgrounds
Limit distractions and maintain privacy by blurring or changing your background during your call or meeting.
[ Manage video ](https://help.webex.com/lw2dx4/)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Meet_your_way.png)
### Meet your way
Webex has tools to keep everyone awake, engaged, and productive—together and apart.
[Record your meeting](https://help.webex.com/n62735y/)  
[Use gestures and reactions](https://help.webex.com/bxg7wz/)  
[Draw it out on a whiteboard](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/3kqmcsb3g66j8mbmzs8qnct/Spark_creativity_with_flexibility_Quick_Start_Guide)
##  Admin launch kits 
Once you’ve got the basics, use these handy kits to roll out features that level up your meeting experience.
[ Meetings launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/gpbrcc4sgxbgttkqs7sswk)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/launch_kit_black.png)
[Polls and Q&A](https://resources.webex.com/s/jjq4vm88kzpcjc9kbh88qqn8)
Make meetings interactive with Slido polling, Q&A, word clouds, and more.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/launch_kit_black.png)
[Breakout sessions](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/at/x4m69t2b8xk54v5ztsg7hpt/Breakout_sessions_QSG_final.pdf)
Use breakout sessions to meet in smaller groups outside the main meeting.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/launch_kit_black.png)
[Webex Assistant](https://resources.webex.com/s/xg88ppkn9q8zrwfbj2jp6mh)
With a voice command, Webex Assistant tracks notes and action items for you.

# Say it in a message
[View the guide](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/fgttf89b2j2wggf8vhnqqq8m/Webex_Messaging_Workload_QSG)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Messaging_hero.png)
testing

##  Begin with the basics 
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/messaging_black.png)
[Read messages](https://help.webex.com/xdgabi/)
You can even peek before you’re ready to reply.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/send.png)
[Send messages](https://help.webex.com/landing/ld-n0bl93g-WebexApp/Message#Send-messages)
Get their attention with formatting, emojis, and animated GIFs.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Create_join_spaces.png)
[Start or join spaces](https://help.webex.com/hk71r4/)
Find a space to join or start your own on a topic or project.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/file-spreadsheet.png)
[Organize messages](https://help.webex.com/landing/ld-n0bl93g-WebexApp/Message#Organize-your-messages)
Filter your messages to find and focus on what’s important.
* * *
## Work smarter with   
AI Assistant
[ See quick start guide ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/j985b74bxfvhjkkmrrstjc7t/AI_Assistant_Messaging_QSG)
[Message summaries](https://help.webex.com/article/nqvghfx/Webex-App-|-Summarize-your-message-threads)
Recap a message thread or an entire space to catch up quickly.
[Message rewrite](https://help.webex.com/article/7bgx73/Webex-App-|-Rewrite-your-messages-with-AI-Assistant)
Adjust your tone, fix your grammar, and perfect your content.
[Live translation](https://help.webex.com/article/38383eb/Webex-App-|-Translate-messages-in-a-space)
Translate a message or an entire space with just a click.
##  Clear communication 
@mentions
Use @all or @mention in a space to get their attention. If they aren’t in the space, they’ll be added to it automatically.
[ Try it out ](https://help.webex.com/p5k20o/)
Reply in threads
Work moves fast! Keep conversations together by replying to a message or thread.
[ Talk it out ](https://help.webex.com/nsjyarr/)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Messaging_UI.png)
### Find what you need faster
Use these tools to stay organized and quickly find people, spaces, and important messages.
[Find people, spaces, messages, files, and devices](https://help.webex.com/article/iecbvc)  
[Create sections for spaces](https://help.webex.com/uaayuo/)  
[Flag important messages](https://help.webex.com/9hf3oc)
##  Admin launch kits 
Once you’ve got the basics, use these handy kits to roll out features that level up your messaging experience.
[ Messaging launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/wt4fm32kjzvvwhnjp87zxcjz)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/launch_kit_black.png)
[Use your favorite apps](https://resources.webex.com/s/phb95wjw7c6758xmkg4bxjvh)
Integrate apps into meetings and spaces for a seamless work experience.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/launch_kit_black.png)
[Share and edit files](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/wbn9wtmxbmg53kq57h4vnt4t/Bring_Your_Projects_to_Life_Admin_Quick_Start_Guide)
Connect your content management platform to work without leaving the app.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/launch_kit_black.png)
[Create community](https://help.webex.com/r78evy/)
Use spaces to build connections and community in a hybrid workforce.
##  What's next? 
Get started
Quickly learn the basics like personalizing your Webex, starting meetings from anywhere, and more.
[ Let's go ](https://adopt.webex.com/use-the-webex-suite/do-more/meet-the-suite "Meet the Suite")
Best practices
At Webex, we do this every day! Follow our expert tips and tricks so you show up your best.
[ Learn more ](https://adopt.webex.com/use-the-webex-suite/do-more/best-practices "Best practices")
Hybrid work
Work doesn’t always happen in one place. We’ll show you how to switch between office, home, or wherever—seamlessly.
[ See how ](https://adopt.webex.com/use-the-webex-suite/do-more/hybrid-work "Hybrid work")

## Simpler and smarter
Simplify interactions with AI features for seamless call transfers, context retention, and agent support.
[ View the guide ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/ngcrpgrgkfb45hgrk4q9hm56/Get_started_with_AI_Assistant_for_Webex_Contact_Center)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/contact_center_analyzer3.png)
## Analyze and understand
Mine real-time and historical data to create unique business views that focus on your top priorities with Webex Contact Center Analyzer.
Customize your dashboard with your favorite visualizations. Then set up automatic email reports for the people who need them.
[ Get the guide ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/2zfkrh5nnc7rts9k9jx56k5r/Webex_Contact_Center_Analyzer)
[ Analyzer help ](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/tajemk/Cisco-Webex-Contact-Center-Analyzer-User-Guide)

## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

##  Get their attention 
Make your meetings stand out from the rest. Break the ice, poll the crowd, or host Q&A with Slido.
[ Slido resources ](https://resources.webex.com/webex-essentials/slido)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/More_LP_Poll.png)
[Polling](https://help.webex.com/n0pdj9x/)

![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/More_LP_QA.png)
[Q&A](https://help.webex.com/nsgzhsdb/)

![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/More_LP_Surveys.png)

##  Communicate   
on your time 
Use Vidcast to stay connected—no meeting required.
[ Vidcast resources ](https://resources.webex.com/webex-essentials/vidcast)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/More_LP_record.png)
[Record yourself or your screen](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/3tgp47pc6xx7b2smwrxsssj/Achieve_More_Asynchronously_with_Vidcast_Quick_Start_Guide)
Create short videos for async updates and trainings. Everyone can react and comment on their own schedule.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/More_LP_edit.png)
[Make your message stand out](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/khsr2q687nmgpj7r2mmc7b65/Advanced_Vidcast_Quick_Start_Guide)
Use our tools to trim and share your videos and analytics to show you what’s working.
* * *
## Level up with  
a webinar
Easily set up invites, registration, moderators, breakouts, and more. You can even send post event surveys and view attendee metrics.
[ See how ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/QPKZK0X0/as/xtpfbgxhfn6hnv2w2fxpnfjc/Webex_Webinars_Quick_Start_Guide)

## Design your workspace
Want clearer, smoother meetings? Input your exact room dimensions and we’ll tell you how to optimize your furniture, acoustics, and devices with Cisco Workspace Designer.
[ Try it out ](https://www.webex.com/us/en/workspaces.html)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/101124_Devices_webex-assistant-logo.png)
## “OK Webex, what can you do?”
Meet your AI Assistant for Cisco devices. With just your voice, you can operate your Board, Desk, or Room Series devices hands-free. Check the calendar, join a meeting, adjust settings, and more.
[ See what AI Assistant can do ](https://help.webex.com/article/nljt9yc)

##  The basics 
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Scedule_Meeting_icon.png)
[Schedule](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/tcbx683hf58n6xmnkvwfhk8t/See_How_to_Join_a_Webex_Meeting_and_Share_Content_on_the_Webex_Room_Series_Video)
Schedule meetings from your browser, Outlook, or the app.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Join_Meeting_icon.png)
[Join](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/nmc8qh6hg6pgc9vwf7km44n/Join_a_Webex_Meeting_from_a_Room_Device_Quick_Start_Guide)
Join a meeting with a simple tap of your screen.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Share_screen_icon.png)
[Share](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/pps58jhrx4q3xbf4wxkjf5w/Learn_How_to_Share_Your_Screen_Using_a_Cisco_Room_Device_Quick_Start_Guide)
No adapter required! Share a file or your screen wirelessly.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/calls_icon.png)
[Call](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/xb8hhb3qbtbhqpt9qxb9grc/Calling_From_a_Cisco_Room_Device_Quick_Start_Guide)
Tap to call any contact or dial a number.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/101124_Devices_webex-assistant-logo.png)
## “OK Webex, what can you do?”
Meet your AI Assistant for Cisco devices. With just your voice, you can operate your Board, Desk, or Room Series devices hands-free. Check the calendar, join a meeting, adjust settings, and more.
[ See what AI Assistant can do ](https://help.webex.com/article/nljt9yc)
## Stay focused
AI-driven camera modes improve the view and enhance your meeting experience.
[People focus dynamically adjusts layout](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/2srv94hxhjfg5r4376pjj4x/People_Focus_Quick_Start_Guide)   
[Frames mode ensures everyone is seen](https://help.webex.com/article/nr3adaf/Frames-mode-for-Board-and-Room-series#sx10_t_people-frames-with-people-focus)
[ Explore camera modes ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/s4x4tn647r4c2pw76jmk4jbh/Camera_modes_for_devices_QSG)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Camera_Intelligence_with_Cisco_Devices_GIF.gif)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/071423_Devices_meet_your_way.png)
### Meet your way
Join a Webex, Microsoft Teams, Google Meet, or Zoom meeting with the touch of a screen. We’ve got a launch kit for every setup.
[Webex](https://resources.webex.com/s/x5mj8qx3wg6sgg5znhhfk6ns)  
[Microsoft Teams](https://resources.webex.com/s/b5ghr74gjxf6nkq4489tnxgp)  
[Google Meet](https://resources.webex.com/s/hrvkrn9skvhmg59jfq38grmn)   
[Zoom](https://resources.webex.com/s/pjg4k5cz6qg5rk7pvxrfjg)
### Show and tell
Easily share your ideas across Cisco devices using Webex, AirPlay or Miracast.
[Share with Webex](https://help.webex.com/pceb9t/)  
[Share with AirPlay](https://help.webex.com/ndlsg3c/)  
[Share with Miracast](https://help.webex.com/kfdwufb/)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/071323_Devices_show_and_tell.png)
##  Engage and inspire 
Collaborate on the fly
Easily add teammates to a call or a meeting that’s in progress with a tap of your screen.
Draw it out
Whiteboard, share your screen, and annotate to keep everyone on the same page.

## Cisco devices for  
Microsoft Teams Rooms
Whether you’re just getting to know your device or setting them up for your users, we’ve got you covered with quick how-to’s and shareable guides.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/DX4devices_LP2_hero_img.png)
  * [ Get started ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/devices/get-started)
  * [ Cisco Rooms ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/devices/cisco-rooms)
  * [ Microsoft Teams Rooms ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/devices/microsoft-teams-rooms)
  * [ Cisco Phones ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/devices/cisco-phones)


  * [Admin guidebooks](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-50960219)
  * [Control Panel](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-63531640)
  * [For users](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-59096044)


### Your guide to any-stage adoption
No matter where you are in your adoption journey, we’ve got use cases, templates, and strategies to help you on your way.
[ Need more technical help? ](https://roomos.cisco.com/doc/MTR)
[![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Jan25_Guidebooks_Plan.png)](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/nxj7qnf9htspw74x6cv83qbp/Devices_Adoption_-_Plan_Guidebook)
[Plan](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/nxj7qnf9htspw74x6cv83qbp/Devices_Adoption_-_Plan_Guidebook)
Define your goals and set up Microsoft Teams Rooms.
* * *
[![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Jan25_Guidebooks_Launch.png)](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/t4q7vkjkfkp7k2xt4fcfz/Devices_Adoption_-_Launch_Guidebook)
[Launch](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/t4q7vkjkfkp7k2xt4fcfz/Devices_Adoption_-_Launch_Guidebook)
Onboard and train users, build champions, and track your success.
* * *
[![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Jan25_Guidebooks_Grow.png)](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/vhgn43x4mnftrctpw6kb5s/Devices_Adoption_-_Grow_Guidebook)
[Grow](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/vhgn43x4mnftrctpw6kb5s/Devices_Adoption_-_Grow_Guidebook)
Maximize your investment with advanced features and integrations.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/DX_for_Devices_screen_with_hand.png)
## Control Panel
Manage your device and adjust settings fast with the control panel. Swipe from the right side of your Cisco device to find it.
[ See how ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/at/hvnq9cx7jm8726qrcm6h4r/Control_Panel_Basics_on_Cisco_devices.pdf#page=4)
What would you like to do next?
Pick what you'd like to learn more about and we'll quickly show you how.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Scedule_Meeting_icon.png) Schedule
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Join_Meeting_icon.png) Join
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Share_screen_icon.png) Share

#### Cisco Phones adoption guide
# Explore
Cisco Desk Phone 9800 Series makes communication simple with secure, flexible, and easy-to-manage features for any workspace.
Explore this page to get an overview of the features and offerings of the 9800 series. Then use the Plan, Launch, and Grow sections to set goals, train your team, and optimize your setup.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/cisco-phones/explore-page-header.png)
  * [ Explore ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/devices/cisco-phones)
  * [ Plan ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/devices/cisco-phones/plan)
  * [ Launch ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/devices/cisco-phones/launch)
  * [ Grow ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/devices/cisco-phones/grow)


  * [More than a phone](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-11933158)
  * [Complete security](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-9101408)
  * [Security for industries](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-49018251)
  * [Action button](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-42576920)
  * [Action button for your industry](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-10965360)
  * [Hot desking](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-21045747)
  * [Easy management](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-58756923)
  * [Better user experience](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-60810330)
  * [Adoption guides](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-43636759)


### More than a phone
The Cisco Desk Phone 9800 Series redefines the desk phone experience. Powered by PhoneOS, they’re easy to set up and manage all in one place. With built-in security and customizable features, these phones go beyond making calls, helping you maximize your investment.
Discover what makes the 9800 Series unique and how to set them up for the best experience.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/cisco-phones/explore-complete-security-new.png)
##  Complete security 
The 9800 Series prioritizes triple-layer security, setting new standards to meet strict [security](https://www.cisco.com/c/dam/en/us/td/docs/solutions/PA/security/9800_security_technical_paper.pdf "security") protocols and protect businesses across industries with [trustworthy devices.](https://www.cisco.com/c/dam/en_us/about/doing_business/trust-center/docs/trustworthy-technologies-datasheet.pdf "trustworthy devices.")
Enable and configure security options to ensure your calling platform operates under strict security and risk management controls, protecting your organization and users.
[Enable security for cloud-managed Cisco phones](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/eyjsp2/Cisco-IP-phone-security-on-9800/8875-\(Multiplatform\) "Enable security for cloud-managed Cisco phones")  
[Enable security for on-prem Cisco phones](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/ntlf2i6/Cisco-IP-Phone-security-on-9800/8875-\(Unified-CM\) "Enable security for on-prem Cisco phones")
##  Security for your industry 
Healthcare
End-to-end encryption and a built-in firewall protect internal and patient communication against data breaches and ensure HIPAA compliance.
Finance
Features like TPM and SELinux secure financial transactions and encrypt sensitive customer and institutional data for PCI DSS regulations.
Government
Cisco Secure Boot verifies hardware, while runtime defenses block malicious code to protect data and maintain security standards.
Retail
PhoneOS security, including chip protection, secure transactions and customer data, preventing fraud and theft.
## A button made for Action
The Action button on the 9800 Series helps teams respond quickly to urgent situations. You can program up to three functions, such as emergency notifications or custom actions, to address specific needs. [Learn more.](https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/how-cisco-phones-enhance-safety-with-emergency-alert-systems/ "Learn more.")
[Configure your action button for cloud-managed Cisco phones](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/n22c0wy/Configure-the-Action-Button-for-9800-\(Control-Hub\) "Enable security for cloud-managed Cisco phones")  
[Configure your action button for on-prem Cisco phones](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/mxwa8f/Configure-the-Action-button-for-9800-\(Unified-CM\) "Enable security for on-prem Cisco phones")
[ See how it works ](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UZFWkAuxvps)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/cisco-phones/new-button-made-for-action.jpg)
##  Action button for your industry 
Healthcare
Page clinical staff or contact authorities quickly during emergencies using the Action button.
Finance
Activate a silent panic alarm and notify teams of security threats using the Action button.
Government
Instantly reach internal and external teams with quick dialing using custom shortcuts on the Action button.
Education
Ensure safety with Alyssa's Law compliance by configuring the Action button to instantly call emergency services.
## Use office space efficiently
Hot Desking made simple: Find an available desk by looking for the green light, sign in to personalize your phone, and easily share resources across hybrid teams.
[ Configure Hot Desking ](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/eb1du4/Configure-Hot-Desking-for-phones-on-Control-Hub)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/cisco-phones/explore-hot-desking-234232.png)
##### See available spaces at a glance
When you enter an office space, the phone’s occupancy lights quickly show where there’s an open desk. Book the desk to get started.
[Take a look](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/r1gu0/LED-indicators-overview "Check it out")
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/cisco-phones/explore-hot-desking-234266.png)
##### Personalize your experience
Sign in, and your phone instantly syncs your contacts, meetings, and settings for a personalized workspace.
[Check it out](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/np9ml9b/Sign-in-to-a-shared-phone-\(Hot-Desking\) "Check it out")
##  Make management easy 
The 9800 Series offers flexible setup, centralized management, and zero-touch deployment.
One phone, any platform
PhoneOS lets you configure 9800 Series phones for cloud or on-premise, saving on future costs if you migrate to Webex Calling.
Simplified deployment
Zero-touch provisioning automates phone setup, whether they're still in the box or already connected.  
  
[Zero-touch provisioning](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/5eomso/Prepare-NFC-onboarding-data-for-Desk-Phone-9800-Series#reference-template_fde23a03-6db7-4814-a6e9-98f69c48f56f "Zero-touch provisioning ")
Centralized management
Easily manage, monitor, and troubleshoot phones in Control Hub. Gain insights with analytics and integrate ThousandEyes to diagnose and fix network issues.
[Get started with Control Hub](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/nkhozs6/Get-started-with-Control-Hub "Get started with Control Hub")  
[Integrate ThousandEyes](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/wy8irv/ThousandEyes-integration-for-Cisco-Desk-Phone-9800-Series-\(Control-Hub\) "Integrate ThousandEyes")
##  Elevate the user experience 
Cisco Phones offer one-touch meetings, clear audio, and a sustainable design for a better, eco-friendly communication experience.
AI noise removal
Enjoy high-definition audio, powerful speakers, and AI noise removal for improved clarity in any workspace.  
  
[AI noise removal](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/nm96shl/Remove-background-noise-for-meetings-and-calls-on-9800/8875 "AI noise removal")
Instantly join meetings
Join Webex Calling meetings instantly with one button to join (OBTJ). Selected models have a built-in calendar button for quick access.  
  
[Cloud: One Button to Join](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/nufqmkg/Check-and-join-a-meeting-on-9800/8875-\(Webex-Calling\) "Cloud: One Button to Join")  
[On-Prem: One Button to Join](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/212ytk/Check-and-join-a-meeting-on-9800/8875-\(Unified-CM\) "On-Prem: One Button to Join")
Accessibility
Features include accessibility options, real-time translation, and voice feedback for audio cues.
[Accessibility features](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/y552uy/Accessibility-features "Accessibility features")[](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/n0rlwxe/Analytics-for-Your-Cloud-Collaboration-Portfolio#CMGT_TK_R833840D_00 "Get started with Control Hub")  
[Real-time translations](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/6aoom1/Use-closed-captions-in-Webex-meetings-on-9800/8875-\(Webex-Calling\) "Real-time translations")
##  Drive adoption 
Whatever stage you’re at, we’ve got use cases, interactive templates, and proven strategies to help drive adoption. We’ll take you on a three-part adoption journey.
Plan
Onboard and implement 
  
Define your business drivers and outcomes, create your adoption team, and ensure technical readiness for successful results with Cisco Phones.  

[Ready to plan your rollout?](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/devices/cisco-phones/plan "Ready to plan your rollout?")
Launch
Use, engage, adopt 
  
Empower users to get started with Cisco Phones’ basic features, improve engagement, and promote adoption.
[Ready to launch?](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/devices/cisco-phones/launch "Ready to launch?")
Grow
Optimize, expand, champion
  
Explore all the ways you can maximize your ROI. Stay updated with the latest releases and help users become pros.
[Get more from Cisco](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/devices/cisco-phones/grow "Get more from Cisco")
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Ellipse-explore-1088-654345.png)
Ready to plan your rollout? 
Now that you’ve seen how the 9800 Series is more than just a phone, it’s time to kick off your adoption journey. Start strong with our step-by-step adoption methodology.
[ Start planning ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/devices/cisco-phones/plan)

# Meet  
Control Hub
Welcome to your home for all things Webex.   
It's where you’ll manage users, access analytics,   
troubleshoot, and more.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CH_pg2_hero.png)
  * [ Get to know Control Hub ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/achieve-more/get-to-know-control-hub)

##  Control Hub fundamentals 
[Verify your domain](https://admin.webex.com/settings#domains-section)
Verify your domain with Webex so you can  
easily claim all your users.
[Auto-assign templates](https://admin.webex.com/manage-users/licenses)
Save time by automatically granting   
licenses to new and existing users.
[Claim your users](https://admin.webex.com/manage-users/users?overlay=showCoachMark)
Claim and add users to your domain to  
make managing them a breeze.
[Claim your domain](https://admin.webex.com/settings#domains-section)
Ensure users are automatically added to  
your organization by claiming your domain.
##  For an even better experience 
[Enable SSO](https://admin.webex.com/settings#authentication-section)
Make sign-on easy and secure for users  
and reduce forgotten password requests.
[Turn on hybrid calendar](https://admin.webex.com/hybrid-services)
Connect mail apps like Outlook or Google   
calendar to make meeting even easier.
[Enable directory sync](https://help.webex.com/article/nj34yk2/Ways-to-add-users-to-your-Control-Hub-organization)
Create a seamless, secure experience by  
connecting to your directory service.
[Brand your experience](https://admin.webex.com/settings#branding-section)
Create a unified look and feel on Webex by  
applying your custom colors and logos.
## AI Assistant is here to help
In Control Hub, the Webex app, or on a Cisco device—learn how you can work smarter and save time with AI.
[ User launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/j7hgtpbx3f9wn6bsctmspw2x)
##### AI Assistant for Control Hub
Get instant answers to your questions, right in Control Hub.
  * [ See how ](https://help.webex.com/article/cp79g9/Ask-the-Cisco-AI-Assistant-questions-in-Control-Hub "See how")


##### AI Foundations online training
Get familiar with AI and how it’s used in our software and devices.
  * [ Enroll ](https://academy.webex.com/learn/courses/2143/webex-ai-foundations-concepts-and-features "Enroll")


##### Control Hub AI online training
Learn how to enable, disable, and manage AI-driven features in Webex Suite.
  * [ Take the course ](https://academy.webex.com/learn/courses/2192/webex-control-hub-artificial-intelligence "Take the course")


![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CH_pg2_prepare_img.png)
##  Prepare for Webex 
Before deploying Webex, you’ll need to prepare your network. Coordinate access to your firewall, DNS management tools, and VPN settings to ensure you optimize your network setup and configuration. Our resources will get you there.
Don’t worry about VPN traffic—users can join meetings without a VPN and you can configure your VPN to direct traffic to the internet or set up tunnels after you configure ports.
  * [ Learn about allowing Webex traffic on your network ](https://help.webex.com/article/WBX264/How-Do-I-Allow-Webex-Meetings-Traffic-on-My-Network "Learn about allowing Webex traffic on your network")
  * [ Explore minimum bandwidth requirements for your Webex services ](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/WBX22158/What-are-the-Minimum-Bandwidth-Requirements-for-Sending-and-Receiving-Video-in-Cisco-Webex-Meetings "Explore minimum bandwidth requirements for your Webex services")
  * [ Check out our bandwidth planning guide ](https://www.cisco.com/c/en/us/products/collateral/conferencing/webex-meetings/white_paper_c11-691351.html "Check out our bandwidth planning guide")

##  Your adoption roadmap 
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CH_pg2_masonry1.png)
Set milestones and celebrate wins

  * [ View milestones ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/66xxm7qsj9m9qr7kwm9k6rc/Onboard_and_implement_Milestones "View milestones")


![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CH_pg2_masonry2.png)

Control Hub makes setting up and managing Webex simple, so you can focus on bigger things, like ensuring your users' success. Our guide will walk you through every step.

![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CH_pg2_masonry3.png)

Once you've configured Control Hub and set up your users, you can use our checklist to make onboarding quick and painless.
  * [ View the checklist ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/8j7bn7kvfjf4n6n4fqvvqsqs/Onboard_and_implement_Checklist "View the checklist")

## Consent Manager
Your opt out preference signal is honored.
## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

# Does hybrid work matter?
The stats say yes, and we're here to guide you through optimizing your Webex services for hybrid working and beyond.
[ Control Hub settings for a hybrid office ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/at/f67fwmqv6s7r2bck858mr/Return_to_Office_Admin_Quick_Start_Guide.pdf)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CH_LP3_hero.png)
  * [ Get to know Control Hub ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/achieve-more/get-to-know-control-hub)
  * [ Hybrid work ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/achieve-more/hybrid-work)
  * [ Flexible workstyles ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/achieve-more/flexible-workstyles)


  * [Video meeting basics](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-45565026)
  * [Save time with messaging](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-273260)
  * [Make it seamless for users](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-20834461)
  * [Next level Webex features](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-26983293)
  * [Share hybrid work tips](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-59928091)
  * [Adoption resources library](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-502922)


![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Product_Detail_pg4_Sect2_Stat1.png)
Keep employees happy
Some employees want to be in an office, but a growing number prefer working remotely. 83% in fact, state that they’d be happier working remotely.*
*State of Remote Work 2019, Owl Labs
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Product_Detail_pg4_Sect2_Stat2.png)
No more churn
Workers are asking to work remotely, and the companies offering remote work are being rewarded—they have 25 percent lower employee churn rates than those without.*
*15 Stats You Need to Know in 2021, Krisp.ai
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Product_Detail_pg4_Sect2_Stat3.png)
Improve your focus
Did you know that working remote improves focus for key tasks by 76 percent?*
*The Me in Team 2021 Report, Atlassian
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CH_LP3_Video_meetings_basics.png)
##  Video meeting basics 
Help your users have more engaging meetings when they turn video or devices on and use custom backgrounds, layouts and gestures. We've got the resources you need to help them look, sound, and interact at their best.
  * [Download the virtual backgrounds launch kit](https://resources.webex.com/s/c5q87fpt8xhqkcbh8rkccx25 "Download the virtual backgrounds launch kit")
  * [Download the gestures launch kit](https://resources.webex.com/s/c735nx73gnff37hggxsx9p "Download the gestures launch kit ")
  * [Download the custom layouts launch kit](https://resources.webex.com/s/8b2qn6r6cf344snrjmfj8gcr "Download the custom layouts launch kit ")
  * [Download the device setup launch kit](https://resources.webex.com/s/kc89bm7kf88qjncnjk7zhsnf "Download the device setup launch kit ")


##  Save time with messaging 
Encourage your users to use spaces, messages, and @mentions to share information and keep work moving. You can share these resources with your users so they can make Webex App work best for them.
TIP: Get your users to download the Webex App
  * [ Download the messaging basics launch kit for your users ](https://resources.webex.com/s/z9g549j6pqrphj5ph4mpbrc "Download the messaging basics launch kit for your users")
  * [ Best practices for messaging ](https://help.webex.com/landing/ld-n0bl93g-WebexApp/Message#Organize-your-messages "Best practices for messaging")


![](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/at/6px95fw83qjj3tz25gs6mbh/webex_create_group_space.gif?auto=webp&format=gif)
##  Make it seamless for users 
While they get on with their work, you can make it easier and more seamless for them.
Webex App
Let users work when, where, and how they want with the Webex App.
  * [ Download the Webex App ](https://www.webex.com/downloads.html "Download the Webex App")


Set up hybrid calendar
Connect calendar services like Outlook or Google calendar to Webex so users can easily schedule and manage meetings.
  * [ Set up hybrid calendar ](https://admin.webex.com/hybrid-services "Set up hybrid calendar")


Meeting styles guide
We get it. We have a lot of options. So we simplified it for you to use what works best for your meeting styles.
  * [ Show me the guide ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/at/5jgmnqbr3j7nq6nqsztj6p93/meeting-style-guide.pdf "Show me the guide")


![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CH_LP3_next_level.png)
##  Next level Webex features 
Every team, business, and organization has a different need. So we put together resources for you to share with your users to suit your meetings, events, and engagement needs.
  * [ Teach users how to use interactive polls in meetings with the Slido launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/jjq4vm88kzpcjc9kbh88qqn8 "Teach users how to use interactive polls in meetings with the Slido launch kit")
  * [ Minimize meetings with Vidcast videos. Download the launch kit to learn how ](https://resources.webex.com/s/bktjbjkwhrhf2s894msxx4k "Minimize meetings with Vidcast videos. Download the launch kit to learn how")

# Flexible workstyles
More employees today work in a hybrid setting and often meetings have at least one remote attendee. As work evolves, support your teams with the flexibility of the Webex Suite.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Admin_Flexible_LP_IMG1_new.png)
  * [ Get to know Control Hub ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/achieve-more/get-to-know-control-hub)
  * [ Hybrid work ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/achieve-more/hybrid-work)
  * [ Flexible workstyles ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/achieve-more/flexible-workstyles)


  * [Start with the best setup](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-17616274)
  * [Support flexible work](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-1024558)
  * [Power up meetings](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-24585305)
  * [Keep work moving](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-49323244)
  * [Share flexible workstyles tips](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-40948344)
  * [Adoption resource library](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-11904312)


## Start with the best setup
Empower your users to work in the way that suits them by adjusting your Control Hub settings. For the best experience, we recommend you enable:
Personal insights  
Closed captioning  
Breakout sessions  
Webex Assistant  
Vidcast  
Enterprise content management  
Embedded apps
We’ve got all of the adoption materials for you to share when it’s time to deploy, too.
Let’s dive in!
## Support flexible work
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Admin_Flexible_LP_IMG2.png)
Closed captioning
Closed captioning provides even more flexibility for inclusive collaboration. Your users can use real-time translation and transcription in more than 100 languages.
  * [ Enable closed captioning ](https://resources.webex.com/s/p85n7c8fq2hhck4p8fmhmp "Enable closed captioning")


![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Admin_Flexible_LP_IMG3.png)
Personal insights
With personal insights, users can use stats and monitoring to help them create healthier habits, better meetings, stronger connections with their most important contacts, and more.
  * [ Enable personal insights ](https://resources.webex.com/s/rq73pspcw7v9j3qngwkw9w98 "Enable personal insights")


## Power up meetings
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Admin_Flexible_LP_IMG4.png)
Breakout sessions
Breakout sessions can be used in workshops, classrooms, or when you need a moment to talk privately with a few participants outside of the main session.
  * [ Learn more about breakout sessions ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/at/x4m69t2b8xk54v5ztsg7hpt/Breakout_sessions_QSG_final.pdf "Learn more about breakout sessions")


![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Admin_Flexible_LP_IMG5.png)
Webex Assistant
With just a voice command, Webex Assistant can take notes and highlight action items so it's easier to manage meetings and keep work moving. And it’s already included in your subscription!
  * [ Enable Webex Assistant ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/xwkcj39pnw35rp7mc9v735/Work_smarter_with_Webex_Assistant_Admin_Quick_Start_Guide "Enable Webex Assistant")


## Keep work moving
Vidcast
With Vidcast, your users can easily create video messages to share with teammates or customers and skip the meeting.
  * [ Set up Vidcast ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/at/5mgsftsb6mznqbbvfqtvz8t/Vidcast_QSG_Admins-FINAL.pdf "Set up Vidcast")
  * [ Download the Vidcast launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/bktjbjkwhrhf2s894msxx4k "Download the Vidcast launch kit")


Enterprise content management
Connect your content management platform to Webex and give your users a more streamlined way to work without leaving the app.
  * [ Set up ECM in Control Hub ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/wbn9wtmxbmg53kq57h4vnt4t/Bring_Your_Projects_to_Life_Admin_Quick_Start_Guide "Set up ECM in Control Hub")
  * [ Download the ECM launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/j93fgn6rxsnb65n4hhkfv8mg "Download the ECM launch kit ")


Embedded apps
With embedded apps, you can make work seamless by easily integrating your existing web applications into Webex meetings and spaces.
  * [ Learn more about embedded apps ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/at/nht6ngkp494hssvrjp3r654/EmbeddedApps_QSG_Admins-FINAL.pdf "Learn more about embedded apps")
  * [ Download the embedded apps launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/cfjjmrjrfbfrjwgg7j5ms2c "Download the embedded apps  launch kit")

# Need help?
We're here for you! Visit our Help Center or explore all the ways you can get what you need.
[ Go to Help Center ](https://help.webex.com/ "Go to Help Center")
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Support_LP_hero_img.png)
##  Resources 
Whether you prefer to help yourself, crowd-source from your peers, or call in a pro, we’ve got you.
Webex status
Check to see if there’s an incident affecting your Webex service.
  * [ Check Webex service status ](https://status.webex.com/service/status "Check Webex service status")


* * *
Chat
Our team is ready to assist you virtually, 24x7.
  * [ Launch chat now ](https://spyder.cisco.com/app/v2/chat?id=MTAwMDA1&source=help.webex.com&CountryInput=GB "Launch chat now")


* * *
Open a case
Get the form you need to open a case with Support.
  * [ Start a new case ](https://mycase.cloudapps.cisco.com/attendeeCaseCreate "Start a new case")


* * *

##  Classes and training 
Our world-class live and recorded classes and trainings will get you started on Webex quickly. If you’re a pro, we’ve got advanced courses too!
[ Explore classes and trainings ](https://adopt.webex.com/resources-and-programs/resources/academy "Explore classes and trainings")
##  Align your goals for the best experience 
You want to make the most of your Webex investment and give users an exceptional experience. That means being able to start quickly and enjoy a platform that meets all your needs. Align your goals with what matters most to you and your users.
Make deployment a breeze
Get step-by-step guidance and checklists in Control Hub that walk you through everything you need to get your users up and running quickly.
[ Get started ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/achieve-more/get-to-know-control-hub "Get to know Control Hub")
Enjoy better hybrid meetings
When work went hybrid, we did too. We’ve got all the resources you need to give your users a great meeting experience—no matter where they are.
[ Better hybrid meetings ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/achieve-more/hybrid-work "Hybrid work")
Get everyone on board
Follow our adoption roadmap with guides and toolkits to ensure you hit all the important milestones along the way.
[ Start your journey ](https://adopt.webex.com/resources-and-programs/resources/methodology "Adoption methodology")
Visit our Adoption Resources Library to build an actionable adoption plan
[ View library ](https://resources.webex.com/portals/webex-admin)

## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

# A launch kit for everything
No matter what product from the Webex portfolio you’re using, we’ve got launch kits to help folks get started. Not sure where to start? Watch this quick video on how to use launch kits, then scroll down to start learning and sharing.
  * [Getting started](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-37559705)
  * [Driving adoption](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-6087688)
  * [Cisco devices](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-19877797)
  * [Webex calling](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-12735847)
  * [Slido](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-31791329)
  * [Vidcast](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-29263996)
  * [Align your goals](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-29749176)
  * [Adoption resources](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-62918510)


##  Getting started 
Calling all admins: these launch kits will get you going in Control Hub and cover the basics for our most popular products. 
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Launch_Kit_LP_1.png)
**Deployment**
Get started with Control Hub and set up the best Webex experience.
  * [ Get to know Control Hub ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/webex-suite/get-started "Get to know Control Hub")


![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Launch_Kit_LP_2.png)
**Webex App and Webex Meetings**
If you’re deploying the Webex App and Meetings together, this is the kit for your users.
  * [ App and Meetings kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/gpbrcc4sgxbgttkqs7sswk "App and Meetings kit")


![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Launch_Kit_LP_3.png)
**Webex Messaging**
This user launch kit focuses on Messaging with Webex. That’s it.
  * [ Messaging launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/wt4fm32kjzvvwhnjp87zxcjz "Messaging launch kit")


![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Launch_Kit_LP_4.png)
**Webex Calling**
Rolling out Calling too? This kit has everything your users need to begin.
  * [ Calling launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/webex-essentials/webex-calling "Calling launch kit")

##  Cisco devices 
Get devices ready for action with Webex and other video conferencing platforms. We’ve got quick start guides, videos, and more.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Launch_Kit_LP_6.png)
Room Series

[ ](https://resources.webex.com/s/trv9f8cgvgm9gqf86p9x3sj8)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Launch_Kit_LP_7.png)
Board and Desk

[ ](https://resources.webex.com/s/wfj54qrt73n78t4m2m6867kh)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Launch_Kit_LP_8.png)
Interoperability
Using Cisco devices with Microsoft Teams, Google Meet or Zoom? There’s a kit for that.
[ ](https://resources.webex.com/s/nnrbvhvppg6qjjtv8qstbchb)
##  Calling launch kits 
Webex Go
Make business calls from your personal phone without revealing your number or getting charged.
  * [ Webex Go launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/7p9r8sj5k2v23ssjxcrh8tr "Webex Go launch kit")


Auto attendant
Set up your auto attendant to cover for you when you have to step away or after-hours.
  * [ Auto attendant launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/mxhwp5jh3snmtjq86qbjg77 "Auto attendant launch kit")


Live answer receptionist
Set up live answer to automate your customer service process and improve the overall customer experience.
  * [ Live answer launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/n8jc9gq3z2x4tjwnp3wtzn5 "Live answer launch kit")


Call queue
Set up call queue for an automatic and seamless call waiting step that suits your organization type, size, and call volume.
  * [ Call queue launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/nwvwx83k8mjr64prrp5z7ntb "Call queue launch kit")


## Slido
It can be challenging to keep everyone’s attention in virtual meetings—we get it! Make your meetings stand out from the rest with Slido polling, Q&A, word clouds, and quizzes. Slido is already included in your Webex subscription. Download this kit to get started.
[ Slido launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/nk4mmwgf9tvwv7q5f5p87tx)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Launch_Kit_LP_9.png)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Launch_Kit_LP_10.png)
## Vidcast
Vidcast is a tool included in Webex that powers asynchronous work. Instead of spending your workday in back-to-back meetings, you can use Vidcasts to get time back in your day.
Record personalized video messages to share team updates, or create polished demos and how-to videos that can be shared time and time again—all without a meeting.
[ Get the Vidcast launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/mzx5mhqc3gpmqvkw43cw6zfs)
##  Align your goals for the   
best experience 
You want to make the most of your Webex investment and give users an exceptional experience, which means being able to start quickly and enjoy an easy-to-use platform that meets all your needs. Align your goals with what matters most to you and your users.
Getting started can be simple
Get step-by-step guidance and checklists in Control Hub that walk you through everything you need to get your users up and running quickly.
[ Get started ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/achieve-more/get-to-know-control-hub "Get to know Control Hub")
Enjoy better hybrid meetings
When work went hybrid, we did too. We’ve got all the resources you need to give your users a great meeting experience—no matter where they are.
[ Better hybrid meetings ](https://adopt.webex.com/deploy-and-manage/achieve-more/hybrid-work "Hybrid work")

##  How will you use Webex? 
##### Modernize meetings

  * [ Express yourself ](https://help.webex.com/article/bxg7wz/Webex-App-%7C-Use-reactions-in-your-meetings "Express yourself")
  * [ Optimize your view ](https://help.webex.com/article/n4f1ptt/Webex-App-%7C-Change-your-video-layout-during-a-meeting "Optimize your view")
  * [ Use breakout rooms ](https://help.webex.com/nroo6fs/ "Use breakout rooms")


* * *
##### Be inclusive
  * [ Enable closed captions ](https://help.webex.com/article/lzi8h2/Show-or-hide-automated-closed-captions-during-a-Webex-meeting-or-webinar "Enable closed captions")
  * [ Translate in real-time ](https://help.webex.com/article/7dzhht/Real-time-translation-in-Webex-Meetings-and-Webex-Events-\(new\) "Translate in real-time")

##  What's next? 
Get started
  
Quickly learn the basics like personalizing your Webex, starting meetings from anywhere,   
and more.
[ Let's go ](https://adopt.webex.com/use-the-webex-suite/do-more/meet-the-suite "Meet the Suite")
Best practices
  
At Webex, we do this every day! Follow our expert tips and tricks so you show up your best.
[ Learn more ](https://adopt.webex.com/use-the-webex-suite/do-more/best-practices "Best practices")
Hybrid work
  
Work doesn’t always happen in one place. We’ll show you how to switch between office, home, or wherever— seamlessly.

## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

##  The basics 
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/calls_icon.png)
[Make calls](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/9kx9h9hw6bvfnhvggqwbx95/How_to_make_a_call_Quick_Start_Guide)
Look for the audio or video call icons throughout the Webex App.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/answer_calls_icon.png)
[Answer calls](https://help.webex.com/xga73p/)
Accept or decline a call with a tap of a button on any device.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/transfer_calls_icon.png)
[Transfer calls](https://help.webex.com/5er4f3/)
Easily send an active call to someone else.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/call-forward_icon.png)
[Forward calls](https://help.webex.com/n6ycytf/)
Forward calls to another number to stay connected when you’re away.
* * *
##  Seamless communication 
Choose your favorites
It’s easy to connect with the people you talk to most. Add your boss, teammates, or customers to your contacts list and call with a click.
[ Add contacts ](https://help.webex.com/z3397j/)
Never miss a call
That’s what voicemail is for! When you have a new voicemail, you’ll see a red icon letting you know how many messages are waiting.
[ Manage voicemail ](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/nzt858bj73j6g7rmhg6qt/Webex_App_voicemail_QSG)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/063023_Calling_UI.png)
### Ready for more?
Use our advanced calling features and make your workday more seamless.
[See all advanced features](https://help.webex.com/nfylvfcb/)  
[Hold and park calls](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/xpts6jw79wrbc5jgwcq7f/Call_hold_and_call_park)  
[Manage multiple lines](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/b9j9wgg2g79ktrpt6gchvp4/Calling_with_multiple_lines_QSG)  
[Record a call](https://help.webex.com/nuu13a1 "Record a call")  
[Review AI-generated summaries](https://help.webex.com/9leme "Review AI-generated summaries")
##  Adoption guides 
Whatever stage you’re at, we’ve got use cases, interactive templates, and proven strategies to help drive adoption.
[![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Plan_2x.png)](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/29mb2kn4rscwsqwrrj34p56/Calling_Adoption_Plan_Guidebook)
[Plan](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/29mb2kn4rscwsqwrrj34p56/Calling_Adoption_Plan_Guidebook)
Define your business outcomes and set up Webex Calling services
* * *
[![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Launch_2x.png)](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/7c6nvj2psrxqk74556p864n5/Calling_Adoption_Launch_Guidebook)
[Launch](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/7c6nvj2psrxqk74556p864n5/Calling_Adoption_Launch_Guidebook)
Onboard and train users, create champions, and track your success
* * *
[![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/Grow_2x.png)](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/wfqrfngg4q9z9bx4c85v3j98/Calling_Adoption_Grow_Guidebook)
[Grow](https://cdn.bfldr.com/YM20Y3NQ/as/wfqrfngg4q9z9bx4c85v3j98/Calling_Adoption_Grow_Guidebook)
Maximize your investment with advanced features and integrations
* * *
##  Admin launch kits 
Once you’ve got the basics, use these handy kits to roll out features that level up your calling experience.
[ Calling launch kit ](https://resources.webex.com/s/chwgkwb4p84c5wkwpwrrpmw)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/launch_kit_black.png)
[Auto attendant](https://resources.webex.com/s/mxhwp5jh3snmtjq86qbjg77)
Enable auto attendant to manage calls when receptionists are unavailable.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/launch_kit_black.png)
[Live answer](https://resources.webex.com/s/n8jc9gq3z2x4tjwnp3wtzn5)
Use live answer to automate your customer service and improve customer experience.
* * *
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/launch_kit_black.png)
[Call queue](https://resources.webex.com/s/nwvwx83k8mjr64prrp5z7ntb)
Set up call queue for a seamless call waiting step that suits your organization and call volume.
* * *
##  What's next? 
Get started
Quickly learn the basics like personalizing your Webex, starting meetings from anywhere, and more.
[ Let's go ](https://adopt.webex.com/use-the-webex-suite/do-more/meet-the-suite "Meet the Suite")
Best practices
At Webex, we do this every day! Follow our expert tips and tricks so you show up your best.
[ Learn more ](https://adopt.webex.com/use-the-webex-suite/do-more/best-practices "Best practices")
Hybrid work
Work doesn’t always happen in one place. We’ll show you how to switch between office, home, or wherever—seamlessly.
[ See how ](https://adopt.webex.com/use-the-webex-suite/do-more/hybrid-work "Hybrid work")

## Video tutorials
Whether you're new or need a refresher, get up to speed fast with this video playlist.
  * [ Agent login ](https://youtu.be/YYDl6zJzi2Y?si=w4tUnzSZkbnctHR1 "Agent login")
  * [ Agent availability ](https://youtu.be/8s7Bn_Zq4uU?si=wKxmkHs18kBGWqia "Agent availability")
  * [ Answer a call ](https://youtu.be/SryqDLNoKTs?si=H3SGXL4hTopLjQKN "Answer a call")


##  Agent basics 
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/082024_CX_img1.png)
#### [Setup](https://help.webex.com/en-us/landing/ld-93x1hg-ContactCenter/Agent?type=complex&subTab=Webex-Contact-Center&category=Get-started)
Welcome to Webex Contact Center Agent Desktop. You’ve got this.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/082024_CX_img2.png)
#### [Calls](https://help.webex.com/en-us/landing/ld-93x1hg-ContactCenter/Agent?type=complex&subTab=Webex-Contact-Center&category=Handle-incoming-calls)
Make and handle calls with expert confidence.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/082024_CX_img3.png)
#### [Digital](https://help.webex.com/en-us/landing/ld-93x1hg-ContactCenter/Agent?type=complex&subTab=Webex-Contact-Center&category=Handle-digital-channel-conversations)
Manage digital customer interactions seamlessly.
## Better together
Get help without having to navigate away from your screen—just click the Webex logo from the header when you’re using Agent Desktop.
  * [ See how ](https://help.webex.com/n444dol/ "See how")
  * [ Get started with Webex Suite ](https://adopt.webex.com/use-the-webex-suite/webex-suite/get-started "Get started with Webex Suite")


![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CC_LP3and4_Collaborate_img.png)
##  Achieve more with AI Assistant 
Let AI Assistant lighten your workload. These features must be enabled by your administrator.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CC_LP3_Achieve_AI_Assistant1.png)
#### [Virtual agent summary](https://help.webex.com/article/ndt475w/Enhance-your-efficiency-using-AI-generated-summaries#concept-template_dc83429d-d22e-4bed-a8b2-be53e8c30bbd)
Review AI Agent summaries so you can resolve calls without repetition.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CC_LP3_Achieve_AI_Assistant2.png)
#### [Dropped call summary](https://help.webex.com/article/ndt475w/Enhance-your-efficiency-using-AI-generated-summaries#concept-template_580e3d84-a5db-4515-a53c-7a9f03b89b8e)
If a call drops, you'll get a summary to easily pick up where you left off.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CC_LP3_Achieve_AI_Assistant3.png)
#### [Wellbeing reminders](https://help.webex.com/7wuj6l/)
Receive automatic reminders to take breaks when you need it most.
## Crush your goals
Step up your game by reviewing your customer interactions and performance reports. Look for opportunities to improve by analyzing your total handled time, wrap-up time, and queue activity.
[ Review your interactions ](https://help.webex.com/en-us/fjya9m/)
[ See your performance data ](https://help.webex.com/n6m0w9z/)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/CC_LP3_Meet_your_goals_img.png)

# Host impactful events
[Watch a demo](https://socio.events/watch-a-demo)
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/020924_Hero_img_Events_LP.png)
  * [ Get started ](https://adopt.webex.com/use-the-webex-suite/webex-suite/get-started)
  * [ Calling ](https://adopt.webex.com/use-the-webex-suite/webex-suite/calling)

  * [Event formats](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-5286538)
  * [Event types](https://adopt.webex.com/#section-40403539)

##  One platform for any format 
[In-person](https://socio.events/solutions/in-person-event-software)
This option is best when all attendees will be physically present in a venue.
[Hybrid](https://socio.events/solutions/hybrid-event-platform)
Hybrid events are flexible, with a mix of in-person and remote attendees.
[Virtual](https://socio.events/products/virtual-event-platform)
When all attendees participate remotely, it’s a virtual event.
##  Scalable for all event types 
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/020924_Sect2_img1_Events_LP.png)
[Conferences](https://socio.events/solutions/conference-software)
Bring your conference to life with branding, multiple tracks, and more.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/020924_Sect2_img2_Events_LP.png)
[Trade shows](https://socio.events/solutions/trade-show-software)
Connect attendees to interactive content and uplevel the exhibitor experience.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/020924_Sect2_img3_Events_LP.png)
[Internal events](https://socio.events/solutions/corporate-event-software)
Drive engagement and bonding across your teams with chat, polls, and Q&A.
##  Ready to get started? 
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/More_LP_Elevate_Events_ICON.png)
[Build your event](https://help.socio.events/articles/3346504-welcome-to-the-webex-events-platform)  
Drive participation with multi-track agendas, streaming, and networking.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/021324_Events_Custom_registration_icon.png)
[Custom registration](https://help.socio.events/collections/2448657-registration)  
Build a branded registration site with flexible ticketing options.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/061423_TRAINING_Webex_suite_LP_icon.png)
[Engage audiences](https://help.socio.events/collections/2725800-for-fun)  
Make it interactive with chat, polls, and gamification.
![](https://adopt.webex.com/Essentials/images/More_LP_Data_ICON.png)
[Data and insights](https://help.socio.events/articles/4701463-event-app-and-community-metrics)  
Improve your events with actionable, real-time insights.
##  Set up for success 
Guides
Browse our guides for event professionals and take your events to the next level.
[ See how ](https://socio.events/resources/guides)
Templates
Save time building your event by using templates created by our event professionals.
[ Dive in ](https://resources.webex.com/s/gxpvcx4nwk9vvg9s4jc6nb8m)
Case studies
Read how our customers are achieving their goals with memorable events.
[ Explore ](https://socio.events/customer-stories)
